&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：P0-C — 在 cuda:3 提交 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct eval (aligned + para_aligned)

### 目标
对 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct（RL post-trained 模型）做 aligned + para_aligned eval，作为 RL observational 验证。不需要训练——这是 inference-only eval。

### 背景
- 服务器：cqa1-21977
- GPU：cuda:3（空闲）
- 项目路径：`/root/autodl-tmp/contamcal-irt-calibration/`
- 目的：验证 signal pattern 在 RL 后模型上是否变化，添加生态验证维度
- Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct 是官方 HF 模型，不需要 LoRA adapter

### 步骤

1. **SSH 到 cqa1-21977**，确认 cuda:3 空闲 + 模型是否已下载：
```bash
nvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,memory.used,utilization.gpu --format=csv,noheader -i 3
# 检查模型是否已缓存
ls -d /root/autodl-tmp/models/*3B*Instruct* 2>/dev/null
ls -d /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/models--Qwen--Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct 2>/dev/null
python3 -c "from transformers import AutoTokenizer; t = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained('Qwen/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct', cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache'); print('cached')" 2>/dev/null && echo "MODEL_CACHED" || echo "NEED_DOWNLOAD"
```

2. **如果需要下载**（用 ModelScope 优先）：
```bash
source /etc/network_turbo
pip install modelscope -q 2>/dev/null
python3 -c "
from modelscope import snapshot_download
snapshot_download('Qwen/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct', cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache')
"
```

3. **检查 evaluate.py 是否支持无 adapter eval**（Instruct 模型不需要 LoRA）：
```bash
grep -n "adapter\|lora\|--model-path\|--adapter-path" /root/autodl-tmp/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py | head -20
python3 /root/autodl-tmp/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py --help 2>&1 | head -30
```

如果 evaluate.py 必须有 --adapter-path，需要确认可以传空或 None。
如果不支持无 adapter 模式，需要修改 evaluate.py 添加支持（让 --adapter-path 变成 optional）。

4. **提交 aligned eval**（用 `submit_training_job`）：
   - 输出目录：`/root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct_aligned/`
   - CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3
   - 4 benchmarks: mmlu, gsm8k, arc_challenge, humaneval
   - 不传 --adapter-path（直接用 base Instruct model）
   - 注意：Instruct 模型已有 chat template，eval 格式可能需要调整

5. **如果 aligned eval 能快速完成**（Instruct 3B eval ~2-3h），可以在同一个 submit 中 batch aligned + para_aligned。否则先提交 aligned。

6. **创建 registry 条目**：
   - exp_id: exp_001_eval_3B_instruct_aligned
   - hypothesis: "Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct (RL post-trained) eval. P0-C RL observational: verify signal patterns after RLHF. No training, inference only."
   - 完整 spec

### 注意
- Instruct 模型的 chat template 可能影响 eval format（特别是 MC 题目）
- GSM8K: Instruct 模型天然支持 CoT，应该表现更好
- 不要用 LoRA adapter，直接 eval base Instruct model
- 如果 evaluate.py 不支持无 adapter 模式，先修改代码再提交

Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. 4. **Paraphrases**: at `/root/autodl-tmp/exp001/paraphrases/` with all 4 benchmark files

My plan is to download Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct via ModelScope to the cache directory, then run the aligned evaluation first without paraphrases, followed by the paraphrase-aligned variant.

I'm thinking through whether to download the model separately or bundle it with the eval command itself. The ModelScope download will cache to `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct/`, and I can load it via the standard transformers API with `from_pretrained('Qwen/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct')`. I'll combine the download and eval into a single command to streamline the process. I'm realizing the cache format difference is the issue—ModelScope creates `Qwen/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct/` while HuggingFace expects `models--Qwen--Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct/`. I could either pass the ModelScope-downloaded path directly as a local path to evaluate.py, or reorganize it into HF's cache format.

Looking at what's already on disk, both formats exist for the 0.5B model, so I'll follow that pattern and download the 3B-Instruct version via ModelScope to `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct`, then use that path for evaluation.

I should also check the existing eval outputs to understand how the mc_format naming convention works for "aligned + para_aligned".

{
  "server": "cqa1-21977",
  "command": "cat > /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/scripts/run_instruct_3b_eval.sh << 'SCRIPT'\n#!/bin/bash\nset -e\n\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh\nconda activate base\nsource /etc/network_turbo\n\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1\nexport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3\n\nMODEL_DIR=/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct\n\n# Download model if not cached\nif [ ! -d \"$MODEL_DIR\" ] || [ -z \"$(ls -A $MODEL_DIR 2>/dev/null)\" ]; then\n    echo \"Downloading Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct via ModelScope...\"\n    python3 -c \"\nfrom modelscope import snapshot_download\nsnapshot_download('Qwen/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct', cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/models')\nprint('MODEL_DOWNLOAD_DONE')\n\"\nelse\n    echo \"Model already cached at $MODEL_DIR\"\nfi\n\nEVAL_SCRIPT=/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py\nRESULTS_BASE=/root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results\n\n# Aligned eval\necho \"=== Starting aligned eval ===\"\npython3 $EVAL_SCRIPT \\\n    --model-path $MODEL_DIR \\\n    --output-dir $RESULTS_BASE/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct_aligned \\\n    --mc-format aligned \\\n    --benchmarks mmlu gsm8k arc_challenge humaneval\n\n# Para-aligned eval\necho \"=== Starting para_aligned eval ===\"\npython3 $EVAL_SCRIPT \\\n    --model-path $MODEL_DIR \\\n    --output-dir $RESULTS_BASE/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct_para_aligned \\\n    --mc-format aligned \\\n    --paraphrase-dir /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/paraphrases \\\n    --benchmarks mmlu gsm8k arc_challenge humaneval\n\necho \"=== All evals done ===\"\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/scripts/run_instruct_3b_eval.sh"
}
